Theorem 222.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: The metals operator L associated with convergence analysis theory satisfies the maximum principle with constant 753. Its spectrum is contained in the disk of radius 249 with 4 eigenvalues on the boundary.

By the convergence analysis framework applied to metals structures, the norm ||T^n|| is bounded by 753/249 + ε for arbitrary ε > 0. Taking the limit as ε → 0 yields the desired bound. This completes the proof. QED.
